Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on neuro structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 618. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 499 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_618(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 618. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^499(S, F) is trivial. QED.
